- **handoff**: Write a compact, evidence-typed handoff for a future agent or session. Use when ending or stopping mid-task, switching context, wrapping up for the day, approaching compaction, or when the user says "/handoff", "wrap up", "pick this up tomorrow/later", "before I close this", or "write up where we are." Preserves the objective and acceptance contract, authority and scope, verified state with evidence pointers, decisions, artifacts, live hypotheses, attempt ledger, failed routes and re-entry conditions, unresolved contradictions, open obligations, and the exact next bounded action. - **handoff**: Write HANDOFF.md capturing the exact state of a work session so the next session, the next agent, or the user next week can resume without re-discovering anything. Use this skill whenever the user sends /handoff, says "wrap up", "save where we are", "write a handoff", "I'm done for today", "pass this to the next agent", or a long coding session is ending with work half-finished. Also use /handoff resume, or any time a session starts in a repo that already has a HANDOFF.md, to pick the work back up and verify the recorded state still matches reality. ([realtripleg/gunners-toolbox](https://github.com/realtripleg/gunners-toolbox/tree/HEAD/skills/handoff))

- **handoff-notes**: The Agent hand for leave state a cold successor can resume. You write the done-condition first, take one bounded action per tick, and stop honestly. An agent that never stops becomes an agent that invents work to avoid idling.
